3b:["$","$L123",null,{"data-raw-code":"Initialize actor μ_θ, critic Q_φ, target networks μ_{θ⁻}, Q_{φ⁻}\nInitialize replay buffer D\n\nFor each step t:\n  # Action selection with exploration noise\n  aₜ = μ_θ(sₜ) + εₜ,   εₜ ~ N(0, σ²)\n\n  # Environment step, store transition\n  Execute aₜ, observe rₜ₊₁, sₜ₊₁\n  Store (sₜ, aₜ, rₜ₊₁, sₜ₊₁) in D\n\n  # Sample mini-batch\n  Sample {(sⱼ, aⱼ, rⱼ, s'ⱼ)} from D\n\n  # Critic update (DQN-style TD target)\n  yⱼ = rⱼ + γ Q_{φ⁻}(s'ⱼ, μ_{θ⁻}(s'ⱼ))\n  Minimize Σⱼ (yⱼ - Q_φ(sⱼ, aⱼ))²  over φ\n\n  # Actor update (DPG theorem)\n  Maximize Σⱼ Q_φ(sⱼ, μ_θ(sⱼ))  over θ\n  (gradient: ∇_θ μ_θ(sⱼ) · ∇_a Q_φ(sⱼ, a)|_{a=μ_θ(sⱼ)})\n\n  # Soft target updates\n  θ⁻ ← τθ + (1-τ)θ⁻\n  φ⁻ ← τφ + (1-τ)φ⁻\n","children":["$","code",null,{"style":{"fontFamily":"var(--font-mono)","fontSize":"0.875em","background":"var(--surface-raised)","border":"1px solid var(--border-subtle)","borderRadius":"var(--radius-xs)","padding":"1px 5px","color":"var(--accent)"},"children":"Initialize actor μ_θ, critic Q_φ, target networks μ_{θ⁻}, Q_{φ⁻}\nInitialize replay buffer D\n\nFor each step t:\n  # Action selection with exploration noise\n  aₜ = μ_θ(sₜ) + εₜ,   εₜ ~ N(0, σ²)\n\n  # Environment step, store transition\n  Execute aₜ, observe rₜ₊₁, sₜ₊₁\n  Store (sₜ, aₜ, rₜ₊₁, sₜ₊₁) in D\n\n  # Sample mini-batch\n  Sample {(sⱼ, aⱼ, rⱼ, s'ⱼ)} from D\n\n  # Critic update (DQN-style TD target)\n  yⱼ = rⱼ + γ Q_{φ⁻}(s'ⱼ, μ_{θ⁻}(s'ⱼ))\n  Minimize Σⱼ (yⱼ - Q_φ(sⱼ, aⱼ))²  over φ\n\n  # Actor update (DPG theorem)\n  Maximize Σⱼ Q_φ(sⱼ, μ_θ(sⱼ))  over θ\n  (gradient: ∇_θ μ_θ(sⱼ) · ∇_a Q_φ(sⱼ, a)|_{a=μ_θ(sⱼ)})\n\n  # Soft target updates\n  θ⁻ ← τθ + (1-τ)θ⁻\n  φ⁻ ← τφ + (1-τ)φ⁻\n"}]}]

1f2:T483,import numpy as np

rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
# 5 actions, true Q*; two noisy critics (one systematically high)
A = 5
Q_star = np.array([0.5, 1.0, 0.2, 1.4, 0.8])
Q1 = Q_star + rng.normal(0, 0.15, size=A) + 0.35  # optimistic bias
Q2 = Q_star + rng.normal(0, 0.15, size=A)

def greedy(Q):
    return int(np.argmax(Q))

print("True best action:", greedy(Q_star), "Q*=", Q_star[greedy(Q_star)])
print("Q1 greedy:       ", greedy(Q1), " (overest risk)")
print("min(Q1,Q2) greedy:", greedy(np.minimum(Q1, Q2)))
print(f"  max Q1={Q1.max():.2f}  max min={np.minimum(Q1,Q2).max():.2f}  true max={Q_star.max():.2f}")

def soft_policy(Q, alpha):
    z = Q / alpha
    z = z - z.max()
    p = np.exp(z)
    return p / p.sum()

print("\nSAC-style soft policy π(a) ∝ exp(Q*/α):")
print(f"{'α':>6}  {'entropy':>8}  {'E[Q]':>8}  {'p(best)':>8}")
best = greedy(Q_star)
for alpha in [0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0]:
    p = soft_policy(Q_star, alpha)
    H = -np.sum(p * np.log(p + 1e-12))
    print(f"{alpha:6.1f}  {H:8.3f}  {p @ Q_star:8.3f}  {p[best]:8.3f}")

print("Notice: twin min prefers safer targets; low α → near-greedy, high α → explore (max-ent).")

227:["$","li",null,{"style":{"marginBottom":"0.35em","fontSize":"1rem"},"children":[["$","strong",null,{"style":{"color":"var(--text-strong)","fontWeight":600},"children":"Exploration mechanisms."}]," DDPG adds external Gaussian action noise; TD3 adds smoothing noise to the ",["$","em",null,{"children":"target"}]," action (critic regularization, not behavior exploration); SAC explores intrinsically through its stochastic policy. For precise, narrow-margin contact, SAC is best because its learned, state-dependent stochasticity can shrink where precision is needed — whereas DDPG's fixed external noise perturbs even precise actions, knocking the gripper off the narrow target and degrading the very precision the task demands."]}]
228:["$","li",null,{"style":{"marginBottom":"0.35em","fontSize":"1rem"},"children":[["$","strong",null,{"style":{"color":"var(--text-strong)","fontWeight":600},"children":"Quadruped setups."}]," (A) 4096 parallel envs yield abundant cheap on-policy data, favoring PPO — on-policy, stable, scales with parallelism, simple. (B) a single robot at ~1 Hz is severely sample-limited, favoring SAC — off-policy and replay-efficient. For (B), add safety: a CBF/shield on actions, Lagrangian constrained RL for the safety stops, a lower entropy target near joint limits, action smoothing, and offline pretraining to avoid hardware-damaging exploration."]}]
